PREFIX  :     <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX  dbpo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>

SELECT  ?resource
WHERE
  { :United_States
              dbpo:capital  ?resource
  }

PREFIX  :     <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX  dct:  <http://purl.org/dc/terms/>
PREFIX  dbprop: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>

SELECT  *
WHERE
  { ?e  dct:subject        <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Category:The_Simpsons_(season_14)_episodes> ;
        dbprop:blackboard  ?chalkboard_gag
  }

SELECT  ?a
WHERE
  { ?a  rdf:type       dbpo:Group ;
        dbprop:origin  dbpr:South_Korea
  }

PREFIX  dbpo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX  rdf:  <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX  gsp:  <http://www.opengis.net/ont/geosparql#>
PREFIX  xsd:  <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX  rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX  foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>
PREFIX  dbprop: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>

SELECT  *
WHERE
  {   { ?city  rdfs:label  "Klaipėda"@sv }
    UNION
      { ?alias  dbprop:redirect  ?city ;
                rdfs:label       "Klaipėda"@sv
      }
    UNION
      { ?alias  dbprop:disambiguates  ?city ;
                rdfs:label            "Klaipėda"@sv
      }
    OPTIONAL
      { ?city  dbpo:abstract  ?abstract }
    OPTIONAL
      { ?city  gsp:lat   ?latitude ;
               gsp:long  ?longitude
      }
    OPTIONAL
      { ?city  foaf:depiction  ?image }
    OPTIONAL
      { ?city  rdfs:label  ?name }
    OPTIONAL
      { ?city  foaf:homepage  ?home }
    OPTIONAL
      { ?city  dbpo:populationTotal  ?population }
    OPTIONAL
      { ?city  dbpo:thumbnail  ?thumbnail }
    FILTER langMatches(lang(?abstract), "sv")
  }

PREFIX  dbpo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX  rdf:  <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX  xsd:  <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX  rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX  foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>
PREFIX  dbprop: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>

SELECT  *
WHERE
  { ?city     rdf:type    dbpo:Place ;
              rdfs:label  "Zapopan"@es .
    ?airport  rdf:type    dbpo:Airport
      { ?airport  dbpo:city  ?city }
    UNION
      { ?airport  dbpo:location  ?city }
    UNION
      { ?airport  dbprop:cityServed  ?city }
    UNION
      { ?airport  dbpo:city  ?city }
      { ?airport  dbprop:iata  ?iata }
    UNION
      { ?airport  dbpo:iataLocationIdentifier  ?iata }
    OPTIONAL
      { ?airport  foaf:homepage  ?airport_home }
    OPTIONAL
      { ?airport  rdfs:label  ?name }
    OPTIONAL
      { ?airport  dbprop:nativename  ?airport_name }
    FILTER ( ( ! bound(?name) ) || langMatches(lang(?name), "es") )
  }

SELECT  *
WHERE
  { dbpr:Afghanistan
              rdf:type  dbpo:Place
    OPTIONAL
      { dbpr:Afghanistan
                  dbpo:capital  ?capital .
        ?capital  grs:point     ?coord
      }
  }

PREFIX  dbpr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>

SELECT  *
WHERE
  { dbpr:United_Kingdom
              rdf:type      dbpo:Place ;
              dbpo:capital  ?capital .
    ?capital  rdf:type      dbpo:PopulatedPlace ;
              grs:point     ?coord
  }

PREFIX  grs:  <http://www.georss.org/georss/>
PREFIX  dbpo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX  rdf:  <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX  dbpr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>

SELECT  *
WHERE
  { dbpr:United_Kingdom
              rdf:type  dbpo:Place
    OPTIONAL
      { dbpr:United_Kingdom
                  dbpo:capital  ?capital .
        ?capital  grs:point     ?coord
      }
  }

PREFIX  rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX  rdf:  <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX  owl:  <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#>

SELECT DISTINCT  ?Dir
WHERE
  { ?Dir  rdfs:label  "Cyprus"@en ;
          rdf:type    owl:Thing
  }

PREFIX  xsd:  <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX  dbpr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>

SELECT  ?p ?o
WHERE
  { <http://dbpedia.org/resource/3_(number)>
              ?p  ?o
    FILTER strstarts(str(?p), "http://dbpedia.org/property/")
  }

PREFIX  xsd:  <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX  rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX  dbpo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX  dbpr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>

SELECT DISTINCT  ?label
WHERE
  { ?Company  ?pharma        dbpr:Pharmaceutical_industry ;
              rdfs:label     ?label ;
              dbpo:abstract  ?abstract
    FILTER langMatches(lang(?label), "en")
    FILTER langMatches(lang(?abstract), "en")
  }

PREFIX  xsd:  <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX  rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX  dbpo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX  dbpr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>

SELECT DISTINCT  ?label
WHERE
  { ?Company  ?pharma        dbpr:Pharmaceutical_industry ;
              rdfs:label     ?label ;
              dbpo:abstract  ?abstract
    FILTER langMatches(lang(?label), "en")
  }

PREFIX  xsd:  <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX  rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX  dbpr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>

SELECT DISTINCT  ?label
WHERE
  { ?Company  ?pharma     dbpr:Pharmaceutical_industry ;
              rdfs:label  ?label
    FILTER langMatches(lang(?label), "en")
  }

SELECT  *
WHERE
  {   { <http://de.dbpedia.org/resource/Olympische_Winterspiele_1924/Bob>
                  ?po  ?x
      }
    UNION
      { ?x  ?pi  <http://de.dbpedia.org/resource/Olympische_Winterspiele_1924/Bob> }
  }
ORDER BY ?pi ?po ?x

SELECT  *
WHERE
  {   { <http://pl.dbpedia.org/resource/Bobsleje_na_Zimowych_Igrzyskach_Olimpijskich_1924>
                  ?po  ?x
      }
    UNION
      { ?x  ?pi  <http://pl.dbpedia.org/resource/Bobsleje_na_Zimowych_Igrzyskach_Olimpijskich_1924> }
  }
ORDER BY ?pi ?po ?x
